+1|/**
+2| * PatchPad Custom DOM-based Text Editor Engine
+3| * Pure custom DOM rendering - no textarea, contenteditable, or external 
editor dependencies.
+4| */
+5|
+6|class PatchPadEditor {
+7|  constructor(container, options = {}) {
+8|    this.container = container;
+9|    this.options = Object.assign({
+10|      lineHeight: 24,
+11|      fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', 'Fira Code', 'Cascadia Code', 'SF 
Mono', Consolas, Menlo, Monaco, monospace",
+12|      fontSize: 14,
+13|      tabSize: 2,
+14|      onDirtyChange: () => {},
+15|      onCursorChange: () => {},
+16|      onHistoryChange: () => {},
+17|      onEscape: () => {},
+18|      onFindRequest: () => {},
+19|      onSaveRequest: () => {}
+20|    }, options);
+21|
+22|    this.lines = [''];
+23|    this.carets = [{ anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 }, head: { line: 0, col: 0 } 
}];
+24|    this.activeCaretIdx = 0;
+25|
+26|    // Measurement & Virtualization
+27|    this.charWidth = 8.4;
+28|    this.lineHeight = this.options.lineHeight;
+29|    this.scrollTop = 0;
+30|    this.scrollLeft = 0;
+31|    this.viewportHeight = 600;
+32|    this.viewportWidth = 800;
+33|
+34|    // Undo / Redo
+35|    this.undoStack = [];
+36|    this.redoStack = [];
+37|    this.lastEditType = null;
+38|    this.lastEditTime = 0;
+39|
+40|    // Find state
+41|    this.findMatchesList = [];
+42|    this.activeFindIndex = -1;
+43|
+44|    // Grapheme Segmenter
+45|    this.segmenter = (typeof Intl !== 'undefined' && Intl.Segmenter)
+46|      ? new Intl.Segmenter('en', { granularity: 'grapheme' })
+47|      : null;
+48|
+49|    // Mouse tracking
+50|    this.isMouseDown = false;
+51|    this.dragStartPos = null;
+52|    this.autoScrollTimer = null;
+53|
+54|    // Caret blink
+55|    this.caretVisible = true;
+56|    this.caretBlinkTimer = null;
+57|
+58|    this.initDOM();
+59|    this.measureFont();
+60|    this.bindEvents();
+61|    this.startCaretBlink();
+62|  }
+63|
+64|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+65|     DOM Initialization
+66|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+67|  initDOM() {
+68|    this.container.classList.add('patchpad-editor-root');
+69|    this.container.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
+70|    this.container.setAttribute('role', 'textbox');
+71|    this.container.setAttribute('aria-multiline', 'true');
+72|    this.container.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Incident Report Content 
Editor');
+73|
+74|    this.container.innerHTML = `
+75|      <div class="patchpad-editor-layout">
+76|        <div class="patchpad-gutter" aria-hidden="true">
+77|          <div class="patchpad-gutter-content"></div>
+78|        </div>
+79|        <div class="patchpad-scroll-view">
+80|          <div class="patchpad-scroll-phantom"></div>
+81|          <div class="patchpad-view-content">
+82|            <div class="patchpad-selection-layer"></div>
+83|            <div class="patchpad-find-layer"></div>
+84|            <div class="patchpad-lines-layer"></div>
+85|            <div class="patchpad-carets-layer"></div>
+86|          </div>
+87|        </div>
+88|      </div>
+89|      <div class="patchpad-font-ruler" 
aria-hidden="true">MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM</div>
+90|    `;
+91|
+92|    this.layoutEl = this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-editor-layout');
+93|    this.gutterEl = this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-gutter');
+94|    this.gutterContentEl = 
this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-gutter-content');
+95|    this.scrollViewEl = 
this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-scroll-view');
+96|    this.scrollPhantomEl = 
this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-scroll-phantom');
+97|    this.viewContentEl = 
this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-view-content');
+98|    this.selectionLayerEl = 
this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-selection-layer');
+99|    this.findLayerEl = this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-find-layer');
+100|    this.linesLayerEl = 
this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-lines-layer');
+101|    this.caretsLayerEl = 
this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-carets-layer');
+102|    this.fontRulerEl = 
this.container.querySelector('.patchpad-font-ruler');
+103|  }
+104|
+105|  measureFont() {
+106|    if (this.fontRulerEl) {
+107|      const rect = this.fontRulerEl.getBoundingClientRect();
+108|      if (rect.width > 0) {
+109|        this.charWidth = rect.width / 40;
+110|      }
+111|      if (rect.height > 0) {
+112|        this.lineHeight = Math.round(rect.height) || 
this.options.lineHeight;
+113|      }
+114|    }
+115|  }
+116|
+117|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+118|     Unicode / Grapheme Cluster Utilities
+119|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+120|  getGraphemes(text) {
+121|    if (!this.segmenter) {
+122|      // Fallback
+123|      return Array.from(text).map((c, i) => ({ segment: c, index: i, 
length: c.length }));
+124|    }
+125|    return [...this.segmenter.segment(text)].map(s => ({
+126|      segment: s.segment,
+127|      index: s.index,
+128|      length: s.segment.length
+129|    }));
+130|  }
+131|
+132|  nextGraphemeCol(line, col) {
+133|    const gs = this.getGraphemes(line);
+134|    for (const g of gs) {
+135|      if (g.index > col) return g.index;
+136|    }
+137|    return line.length;
+138|  }
+139|
+140|  prevGraphemeCol(line, col) {
+141|    const gs = this.getGraphemes(line);
+142|    for (let i = gs.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+143|      if (gs[i].index < col) return gs[i].index;
+144|    }
+145|    return 0;
+146|  }
+147|
+148|  deleteGraphemeBefore(line, col) {
+149|    if (col <= 0) return { newLine: line, newCol: 0 };
+150|    const prevCol = this.prevGraphemeCol(line, col);
+151|    const newLine = line.slice(0, prevCol) + line.slice(col);
+152|    return { newLine, newCol: prevCol };
+153|  }
+154|
+155|  deleteGraphemeAfter(line, col) {
+156|    if (col >= line.length) return { newLine: line, newCol: col };
+157|    const nextCol = this.nextGraphemeCol(line, col);
+158|    const newLine = line.slice(0, col) + line.slice(nextCol);
+159|    return { newLine, newCol: col };
+160|  }
+161|
+162|  isWordChar(c) {
+163|    return c && /[\p{L}\p{N}_]/u.test(c);
+164|  }
+165|
+166|  nextWordCol(line, col) {
+167|    const len = line.length;
+168|    if (col >= len) return len;
+169|    let i = col;
+170|    if (this.isWordChar(line[i])) {
+171|      while (i < len && this.isWordChar(line[i])) i++;
+172|      return i;
+173|    }
+174|    if (!/\s/.test(line[i])) {
+175|      while (i < len && !this.isWordChar(line[i]) && !/\s/.test(line[i])) 
i++;
+176|      return i;
+177|    }
+178|    while (i < len && /\s/.test(line[i])) i++;
+179|    while (i < len && this.isWordChar(line[i])) i++;
+180|    return i;
+181|  }
+182|
+183|  prevWordCol(line, col) {
+184|    if (col <= 0) return 0;
+185|    let i = col;
+186|    while (i > 0 && !this.isWordChar(line[i - 1]) && /\s/.test(line[i - 
1])) i--;
+187|    if (i > 0 && !this.isWordChar(line[i - 1])) {
+188|      while (i > 0 && !this.isWordChar(line[i - 1]) && !/\s/.test(line[i - 
1])) i--;
+189|      return i;
+190|    }
+191|    while (i > 0 && this.isWordChar(line[i - 1])) i--;
+192|    return i;
+193|  }
+194|
+195|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+196|     Content & State Accessors
+197|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+198|  getText() {
+199|    return this.lines.join('\n');
+200|  }
+201|
+202|  setText(text, isExternalLoad = false) {
+203|    const prevText = this.getText();
+204|    const prevCarets = this.cloneCarets();
+205|
+206|    this.lines = text.split('\n');
+207|    if (this.lines.length === 0) this.lines = [''];
+208|
+209|    // Clamp carets
+210|    this.carets = this.carets.map(c => ({
+211|      anchor: this.clampPos(c.anchor),
+212|      head: this.clampPos(c.head)
+213|    }));
+214|
+215|    if (isExternalLoad) {
+216|      this.undoStack = [];
+217|      this.redoStack = [];
+218|      this.carets = [{ anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 }, head: { line: 0, col: 0
} }];
+219|      this.activeCaretIdx = 0;
+220|    } else {
+221|      this.pushUndoSnapshot(
+222|        { text: prevText, carets: prevCarets },
+223|        { text: this.getText(), carets: this.cloneCarets() },
+224|        'restore'
+225|      );
+226|    }
+227|
+228|    this.updateScrollPhantom();
+229|    this.render();
+230|    this.notifyChange();
+231|  }
+232|
+233|  clampPos(pos) {
+234|    const line = Math.max(0, Math.min(pos.line, this.lines.length - 1));
+235|    const maxCol = this.lines[line] ? this.lines[line].length : 0;
+236|    const col = Math.max(0, Math.min(pos.col, maxCol));
+237|    return { line, col };
+238|  }
+239|
+240|  cloneCarets() {
+241|    return this.carets.map(c => ({
+242|      anchor: { line: c.anchor.line, col: c.anchor.col },
+243|      head: { line: c.head.line, col: c.head.col }
+244|    }));
+245|  }
+246|
+247|  comparePos(a, b) {
+248|    if (a.line !== b.line) return a.line - b.line;
+249|    return a.col - b.col;
+250|  }
+251|
+252|  getNormalizedCaret(caret) {
+253|    if (this.comparePos(caret.anchor, caret.head) <= 0) {
+254|      return { start: { ...caret.anchor }, end: { ...caret.head } };
+255|    }
+256|    return { start: { ...caret.head }, end: { ...caret.anchor } };
+257|  }
+258|
+259|  hasSelection(caret = this.carets[0]) {
+260|    return caret.anchor.line !== caret.head.line || caret.anchor.col !== 
caret.head.col;
+261|  }
+262|
+263|  getSelectedText(caret = this.carets[0]) {
+264|    const { start, end } = this.getNormalizedCaret(caret);
+265|    if (start.line === end.line && start.col === end.col) return '';
+266|    if (start.line === end.line) {
+267|      return this.lines[start.line].slice(start.col, end.col);
+268|    }
+269|    const result = [];
+270|    result.push(this.lines[start.line].slice(start.col));
+271|    for (let i = start.line + 1; i < end.line; i++) {
+272|      result.push(this.lines[i]);
+273|    }
+274|    result.push(this.lines[end.line].slice(0, end.col));
+275|    return result.join('\n');
+276|  }
+277|
+278|  getAllSelectedText() {
+279|    const texts = this.carets.map(c => this.getSelectedText(c)).filter(t =>
t.length > 0);
+280|    return texts.join('\n');
+281|  }
+282|
+283|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+284|     Undo / Redo System
+285|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+286|  pushUndoSnapshot(beforeState, afterState, type) {
+287|    const now = Date.now();
+288|
+289|    // Group consecutive single-character typing at same location
+290|    if (
+291|      type === 'type' &&
+292|      this.lastEditType === 'type' &&
+293|      this.undoStack.length > 0 &&
+294|      now - this.lastEditTime < 1500
+295|    ) {
+296|      // Update top undo entry after state
+297|      this.undoStack[this.undoStack.length - 1].after = afterState;
+298|    } else {
+299|      this.undoStack.push({
+300|        before: beforeState,
+301|        after: afterState,
+302|        type: type,
+303|        time: now
+304|      });
+305|    }
+306|
+307|    this.redoStack = [];
+308|    this.lastEditType = type;
+309|    this.lastEditTime = now;
+310|    this.options.onHistoryChange(this.canUndo(), this.canRedo());
+311|  }
+312|
+313|  canUndo() {
+314|    return this.undoStack.length > 0;
+315|  }
+316|
+317|  canRedo() {
+318|    return this.redoStack.length > 0;
+319|  }
+320|
+321|  undo() {
+322|    if (!this.canUndo()) return;
+323|    const entry = this.undoStack.pop();
+324|    this.redoStack.push(entry);
+325|
+326|    this.lines = entry.before.text.split('\n');
+327|    this.carets = entry.before.carets.map(c => ({
+328|      anchor: { ...c.anchor },
+329|      head: { ...c.head }
+330|    }));
+331|    this.lastEditType = null;
+332|
+333|    this.updateScrollPhantom();
+334|    this.scrollToCaret();
+335|    this.render();
+336|    this.notifyChange();
+337|    this.options.onHistoryChange(this.canUndo(), this.canRedo());
+338|  }
+339|
+340|  redo() {
+341|    if (!this.canRedo()) return;
+342|    const entry = this.redoStack.pop();
+343|    this.undoStack.push(entry);
+344|
+345|    this.lines = entry.after.text.split('\n');
+346|    this.carets = entry.after.carets.map(c => ({
+347|      anchor: { ...c.anchor },
+348|      head: { ...c.head }
+349|    }));
+350|    this.lastEditType = null;
+351|
+352|    this.updateScrollPhantom();
+353|    this.scrollToCaret();
+354|    this.render();
+355|    this.notifyChange();
+356|    this.options.onHistoryChange(this.canUndo(), this.canRedo());
+357|  }
+358|
+359|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+360|     Editing Mutations (Multi-Caret Aware)
+361|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+362|  executeMutation(mutationFn, type = 'edit') {
+363|    const beforeText = this.getText();
+364|    const beforeCarets = this.cloneCarets();
+365|
+366|    mutationFn();
+367|
+368|    const afterText = this.getText();
+369|    const afterCarets = this.cloneCarets();
+370|
+371|    this.pushUndoSnapshot(
+372|      { text: beforeText, carets: beforeCarets },
+373|      { text: afterText, carets: afterCarets },
+374|      type
+375|    );
+376|
+377|    this.updateScrollPhantom();
+378|    this.scrollToCaret();
+379|    this.render();
+380|    this.notifyChange();
+381|  }
+382|
+383|  // Inserts text (supports multi-caret and selection replacement)
+384|  insertText(text, editType = 'type') {
+385|    this.executeMutation(() => {
+386|      // Sort carets bottom to top
+387|      const sortedIndices = this.carets.map((c, i) => i).sort((a, b) => {
+388|        const normA = this.getNormalizedCaret(this.carets[a]);
+389|        const normB = this.getNormalizedCaret(this.carets[b]);
+390|        return this.comparePos(normB.start, normA.start);
+391|      });
+392|
+393|      const newCarets = [...this.carets];
+394|
+395|      for (const idx of sortedIndices) {
+396|        const caret = this.carets[idx];
+397|        const { start, end } = this.getNormalizedCaret(caret);
+398|
+399|        // Delete selection span
+400|        const linesBefore = this.lines.slice(0, start.line);
+401|        const startLinePrefix = this.lines[start.line].slice(0, start.col);
+402|        const endLineSuffix = this.lines[end.line].slice(end.col);
+403|        const linesAfter = this.lines.slice(end.line + 1);
+404|
+405|        const textLines = text.split('\n');
+406|        let newPos = { line: 0, col: 0 };
+407|
+408|        if (textLines.length === 1) {
+409|          const mergedLine = startLinePrefix + textLines[0] + 
endLineSuffix;
+410|          this.lines = [...linesBefore, mergedLine, ...linesAfter];
+411|          newPos = { line: start.line, col: startLinePrefix.length + 
textLines[0].length };
+412|        } else {
+413|          const firstLine = startLinePrefix + textLines[0];
+414|          const middleLines = textLines.slice(1, -1);
+415|          const lastLine = textLines[textLines.length - 1] + endLineSuffix;
+416|          this.lines = [...linesBefore, firstLine, ...middleLines, 
lastLine, ...linesAfter];
+417|          newPos = {
+418|            line: start.line + textLines.length - 1,
+419|            col: textLines[textLines.length - 1].length
+420|          };
+421|        }
+422|
+423|        newCarets[idx] = { anchor: { ...newPos }, head: { ...newPos } };
+424|      }
+425|
+426|      this.carets = this.mergeCarets(newCarets);
+427|    }, editType);
+428|  }
+429|
+430|  deleteBackward() {
+431|    this.executeMutation(() => {
+432|      const sortedIndices = this.carets.map((c, i) => i).sort((a, b) => {
+433|        const normA = this.getNormalizedCaret(this.carets[a]);
+434|        const normB = this.getNormalizedCaret(this.carets[b]);
+435|        return this.comparePos(normB.start, normA.start);
+436|      });
+437|
+438|      const newCarets = [...this.carets];
+439|
+440|      for (const idx of sortedIndices) {
+441|        const caret = this.carets[idx];
+442|
+443|        if (this.hasSelection(caret)) {
+444|          // Delete selection
+445|          const { start, end } = this.getNormalizedCaret(caret);
+446|          const linesBefore = this.lines.slice(0, start.line);
+447|          const startLinePrefix = this.lines[start.line].slice(0, 
start.col);
+448|          const endLineSuffix = this.lines[end.line].slice(end.col);
+449|          const linesAfter = this.lines.slice(end.line + 1);
+450|
+451|          this.lines = [...linesBefore, startLinePrefix + endLineSuffix, 
...linesAfter];
+452|          newCarets[idx] = { anchor: { ...start }, head: { ...start } };
+453|        } else {
+454|          const { line, col } = caret.head;
+455|          if (col > 0) {
+456|            // Delete grapheme before col
+457|            const { newLine, newCol } = 
this.deleteGraphemeBefore(this.lines[line], col);
+458|            this.lines[line] = newLine;
+459|            newCarets[idx] = { anchor: { line, col: newCol }, head: { line,
col: newCol } };
+460|          } else if (line > 0) {
+461|            // Merge with previous line
+462|            const prevLineLen = this.lines[line - 1].length;
+463|            this.lines[line - 1] = this.lines[line - 1] + this.lines[line];
+464|            this.lines.splice(line, 1);
+465|            const newPos = { line: line - 1, col: prevLineLen };
+466|            newCarets[idx] = { anchor: newPos, head: newPos };
+467|          }
+468|        }
+469|      }
+470|
+471|      this.carets = this.mergeCarets(newCarets);
+472|    }, 'delete');
+473|  }
+474|
+475|  deleteForward() {
+476|    this.executeMutation(() => {
+477|      const sortedIndices = this.carets.map((c, i) => i).sort((a, b) => {
+478|        const normA = this.getNormalizedCaret(this.carets[a]);
+479|        const normB = this.getNormalizedCaret(this.carets[b]);
+480|        return this.comparePos(normB.start, normA.start);
+481|      });
+482|
+483|      const newCarets = [...this.carets];
+484|
+485|      for (const idx of sortedIndices) {
+486|        const caret = this.carets[idx];
+487|
+488|        if (this.hasSelection(caret)) {
+489|          // Delete selection
+490|          const { start, end } = this.getNormalizedCaret(caret);
+491|          const linesBefore = this.lines.slice(0, start.line);
+492|          const startLinePrefix = this.lines[start.line].slice(0, 
start.col);
+493|          const endLineSuffix = this.lines[end.line].slice(end.col);
+494|          const linesAfter = this.lines.slice(end.line + 1);
+495|
+496|          this.lines = [...linesBefore, startLinePrefix + endLineSuffix, 
...linesAfter];
+497|          newCarets[idx] = { anchor: { ...start }, head: { ...start } };
+498|        } else {
+499|          const { line, col } = caret.head;
+500|          if (col < this.lines[line].length) {
+501|            // Delete grapheme after col
+502|            const { newLine, newCol } = 
this.deleteGraphemeAfter(this.lines[line], col);
+503|            this.lines[line] = newLine;
+504|            newCarets[idx] = { anchor: { line, col: newCol }, head: { line,
col: newCol } };
+505|          } else if (line < this.lines.length - 1) {
+506|            // Merge with next line
+507|            this.lines[line] = this.lines[line] + this.lines[line + 1];
+508|            this.lines.splice(line + 1, 1);
+509|            newCarets[idx] = { anchor: { line, col }, head: { line, col } 
};
+510|          }
+511|        }
+512|      }
+513|
+514|      this.carets = this.mergeCarets(newCarets);
+515|    }, 'delete');
+516|  }
+517|
+518|  indent() {
+519|    const hasMultiline = this.carets.some(c => c.anchor.line !== 
c.head.line);
+520|    if (!hasMultiline && this.carets.length === 1 && !this.hasSelection()) 
{
+521|      this.insertText('  ', 'indent');
+522|      return;
+523|    }
+524|
+525|    this.executeMutation(() => {
+526|      // Find all affected lines
+527|      const affectedLines = new Set();
+528|      for (const caret of this.carets) {
+529|        const { start, end } = this.getNormalizedCaret(caret);
+530|        const endLine = (end.col === 0 && end.line > start.line) ? end.line
- 1 : end.line;
+531|        for (let l = start.line; l <= endLine; l++) {
+532|          affectedLines.add(l);
+533|        }
+534|      }
+535|
+536|      for (const lineIdx of affectedLines) {
+537|        this.lines[lineIdx] = '  ' + this.lines[lineIdx];
+538|      }
+539|
+540|      // Shift carets
+541|      this.carets = this.carets.map(c => ({
+542|        anchor: { line: c.anchor.line, col: c.anchor.col + 2 },
+543|        head: { line: c.head.line, col: c.head.col + 2 }
+544|      }));
+545|    }, 'indent');
+546|  }
+547|
+548|  unindent() {
+549|    this.executeMutation(() => {
+550|      const affectedLines = new Set();
+551|      for (const caret of this.carets) {
+552|        const { start, end } = this.getNormalizedCaret(caret);
+553|        const endLine = (end.col === 0 && end.line > start.line) ? end.line
- 1 : end.line;
+554|        for (let l = start.line; l <= endLine; l++) {
+555|          affectedLines.add(l);
+556|        }
+557|      }
+558|
+559|      const lineShifts = {};
+560|      for (const lineIdx of affectedLines) {
+561|        const line = this.lines[lineIdx];
+562|        let spacesToRemove = 0;
+563|        if (line.startsWith('  ')) spacesToRemove = 2;
+564|        else if (line.startsWith(' ') || line.startsWith('\t')) 
spacesToRemove = 1;
+565|
+566|        if (spacesToRemove > 0) {
+567|          this.lines[lineIdx] = line.slice(spacesToRemove);
+568|          lineShifts[lineIdx] = spacesToRemove;
+569|        }
+570|      }
+571|
+572|      this.carets = this.carets.map(c => {
+573|        const anchorShift = lineShifts[c.anchor.line] || 0;
+574|        const headShift = lineShifts[c.head.line] || 0;
+575|        return {
+576|          anchor: { line: c.anchor.line, col: Math.max(0, c.anchor.col - 
anchorShift) },
+577|          head: { line: c.head.line, col: Math.max(0, c.head.col - 
headShift) }
+578|        };
+579|      });
+580|    }, 'unindent');
+581|  }
+582|
+583|  mergeCarets(carets) {
+584|    if (carets.length <= 1) return carets;
+585|    // Deduplicate identical positions
+586|    const unique = [];
+587|    for (const c of carets) {
+588|      const exists = unique.some(
+589|        u => u.anchor.line === c.anchor.line &&
+590|             u.anchor.col === c.anchor.col &&
+591|             u.head.line === c.head.line &&
+592|             u.head.col === c.head.col
+593|      );
+594|      if (!exists) unique.push(c);
+595|    }
+596|    return unique.length > 0 ? unique : [carets[0]];
+597|  }
+598|
+599|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+600|     Cursor Navigation
+601|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+602|  moveCaret(direction, { shift = false, word = false, doc = false, lineEdge
= false } = {}) {
+603|    this.resetCaretBlink();
+604|
+605|    this.carets = this.carets.map(caret => {
+606|      let head = { ...caret.head };
+607|      const currentLine = this.lines[head.line] || '';
+608|
+609|      if (doc) {
+610|        if (direction === 'up' || direction === 'left') {
+611|          head = { line: 0, col: 0 };
+612|        } else {
+613|          const lastLine = this.lines.length - 1;
+614|          head = { line: lastLine, col: this.lines[lastLine].length };
+615|        }
+616|      } else if (lineEdge) {
+617|        if (direction === 'left') {
+618|          head.col = 0;
+619|        } else if (direction === 'right') {
+620|          head.col = currentLine.length;
+621|        }
+622|      } else if (word) {
+623|        if (direction === 'left') {
+624|          if (head.col === 0 && head.line > 0) {
+625|            head.line--;
+626|            head.col = this.lines[head.line].length;
+627|          } else {
+628|            head.col = this.prevWordCol(currentLine, head.col);
+629|          }
+630|        } else if (direction === 'right') {
+631|          if (head.col >= currentLine.length && head.line < 
this.lines.length - 1) {
+632|            head.line++;
+633|            head.col = 0;
+634|          } else {
+635|            head.col = this.nextWordCol(currentLine, head.col);
+636|          }
+637|        }
+638|      } else {
+639|        // Simple 1-step move
+640|        if (direction === 'left') {
+641|          if (!shift && this.hasSelection(caret)) {
+642|            head = this.getNormalizedCaret(caret).start;
+643|          } else if (head.col > 0) {
+644|            head.col = this.prevGraphemeCol(currentLine, head.col);
+645|          } else if (head.line > 0) {
+646|            head.line--;
+647|            head.col = this.lines[head.line].length;
+648|          }
+649|        } else if (direction === 'right') {
+650|          if (!shift && this.hasSelection(caret)) {
+651|            head = this.getNormalizedCaret(caret).end;
+652|          } else if (head.col < currentLine.length) {
+653|            head.col = this.nextGraphemeCol(currentLine, head.col);
+654|          } else if (head.line < this.lines.length - 1) {
+655|            head.line++;
+656|            head.col = 0;
+657|          }
+658|        } else if (direction === 'up') {
+659|          if (head.line > 0) {
+660|            head.line--;
+661|            head.col = Math.min(head.col, this.lines[head.line].length);
+662|          } else {
+663|            head.col = 0;
+664|          }
+665|        } else if (direction === 'down') {
+666|          if (head.line < this.lines.length - 1) {
+667|            head.line++;
+668|            head.col = Math.min(head.col, this.lines[head.line].length);
+669|          } else {
+670|            head.col = currentLine.length;
+671|          }
+672|        }
+673|      }
+674|
+675|      const anchor = shift ? { ...caret.anchor } : { ...head };
+676|      return { anchor, head };
+677|    });
+678|
+679|    this.scrollToCaret();
+680|    this.render();
+681|    this.notifyCursor();
+682|  }
+683|
+684|  selectAll() {
+685|    const lastLine = this.lines.length - 1;
+686|    this.carets = [{
+687|      anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 },
+688|      head: { line: lastLine, col: this.lines[lastLine].length }
+689|    }];
+690|    this.render();
+691|    this.notifyCursor();
+692|  }
+693|
+694|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+695|     Find and Replace
+696|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+697|  findMatches(query, caseSensitive = false) {
+698|    if (!query) {
+699|      this.findMatchesList = [];
+700|      this.activeFindIndex = -1;
+701|      this.renderFindHighlights();
+702|      return [];
+703|    }
+704|
+705|    const matches = [];
+706|    const flags = caseSensitive ? 'g' : 'gi';
+707|    const escaped = query.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
+708|    const regex = new RegExp(escaped, flags);
+709|
+710|    for (let lineIdx = 0; lineIdx < this.lines.length; lineIdx++) {
+711|      const lineText = this.lines[lineIdx];
+712|      let match;
+713|      while ((match = regex.exec(lineText)) !== null) {
+714|        matches.push({
+715|          line: lineIdx,
+716|          startCol: match.index,
+717|          endCol: match.index + match[0].length,
+718|          text: match[0]
+719|        });
+720|        if (regex.lastIndex === match.index) regex.lastIndex++;
+721|      }
+722|    }
+723|
+724|    this.findMatchesList = matches;
+725|    if (matches.length > 0) {
+726|      if (this.activeFindIndex < 0 || this.activeFindIndex >= 
matches.length) {
+727|        // Find match closest to current primary caret
+728|        const caretPos = this.carets[0].head;
+729|        let bestIdx = 0;
+730|        for (let i = 0; i < matches.length; i++) {
+731|          if (this.comparePos(matches[i], caretPos) >= 0) {
+732|            bestIdx = i;
+733|            break;
+734|          }
+735|        }
+736|        this.activeFindIndex = bestIdx;
+737|      }
+738|    } else {
+739|      this.activeFindIndex = -1;
+740|    }
+741|
+742|    this.renderFindHighlights();
+743|    return matches;
+744|  }
+745|
+746|  goToNextMatch() {
+747|    if (this.findMatchesList.length === 0) return null;
+748|    this.activeFindIndex = (this.activeFindIndex + 1) % 
this.findMatchesList.length;
+749|    return this.selectActiveMatch();
+750|  }
+751|
+752|  goToPrevMatch() {
+753|    if (this.findMatchesList.length === 0) return null;
+754|    this.activeFindIndex = (this.activeFindIndex - 1 + 
this.findMatchesList.length) % this.findMatchesList.length;
+755|    return this.selectActiveMatch();
+756|  }
+757|
+758|  selectActiveMatch() {
+759|    if (this.activeFindIndex < 0 || this.activeFindIndex >= 
this.findMatchesList.length) return null;
+760|    const match = this.findMatchesList[this.activeFindIndex];
+761|    this.carets = [{
+762|      anchor: { line: match.line, col: match.startCol },
+763|      head: { line: match.line, col: match.endCol }
+764|    }];
+765|    this.scrollToCaret();
+766|    this.render();
+767|    this.notifyCursor();
+768|    return { match, index: this.activeFindIndex, total: 
this.findMatchesList.length };
+769|  }
+770|
+771|  replaceCurrentMatch(replacement) {
+772|    if (this.activeFindIndex < 0 || this.activeFindIndex >= 
this.findMatchesList.length) return false;
+773|    const match = this.findMatchesList[this.activeFindIndex];
+774|
+775|    this.carets = [{
+776|      anchor: { line: match.line, col: match.startCol },
+777|      head: { line: match.line, col: match.endCol }
+778|    }];
+779|
+780|    this.insertText(replacement, 'replace');
+781|
+782|    return true;
+783|  }
+784|
+785|  replaceAllMatches(query, replacement, caseSensitive = false) {
+786|    if (!query) return 0;
+787|    const matches = this.findMatches(query, caseSensitive);
+788|    if (matches.length === 0) return 0;
+789|
+790|    const count = matches.length;
+791|
+792|    this.executeMutation(() => {
+793|      const flags = caseSensitive ? 'g' : 'gi';
+794|      const escaped = query.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
+795|      const regex = new RegExp(escaped, flags);
+796|
+797|      this.lines = this.lines.map(line => line.replace(regex, 
replacement));
+798|      this.carets = [{ anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 }, head: { line: 0, col: 0
} }];
+799|    }, 'replace-all');
+800|
+801|    this.findMatches(query, caseSensitive);
+802|    return count;
+803|  }
+804|
+805|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+806|     Virtual Scrolling & Geometry
+807|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+808|  updateScrollPhantom() {
+809|    const totalHeight = Math.max(this.lines.length * this.lineHeight, 
this.viewportHeight);
+810|    this.scrollPhantomEl.style.height = `${totalHeight}px`;
+811|
+812|    // Measure max line width for horizontal scroll
+813|    let maxCols = 80;
+814|    const sampleStep = Math.max(1, Math.floor(this.lines.length / 50));
+815|    for (let i = 0; i < this.lines.length; i += sampleStep) {
+816|      if (this.lines[i].length > maxCols) maxCols = this.lines[i].length;
+817|    }
+818|    const totalWidth = Math.max(maxCols * this.charWidth + 100, 
this.viewportWidth);
+819|    this.scrollPhantomEl.style.width = `${totalWidth}px`;
+820|  }
+821|
+822|  getVisibleRange() {
+823|    const buffer = 15;
+824|    const startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(this.scrollTop / 
this.lineHeight) - buffer);
+825|    const endLine = Math.min(
+826|      this.lines.length - 1,
+827|      Math.ceil((this.scrollTop + this.viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) +
buffer
+828|    );
+829|    return { startLine, endLine };
+830|  }
+831|
+832|  scrollToCaret(caret = this.carets[0]) {
+833|    const pos = caret.head;
+834|    const targetY = pos.line * this.lineHeight;
+835|    const targetX = pos.col * this.charWidth;
+836|
+837|    const viewH = this.scrollViewEl.clientHeight || this.viewportHeight;
+838|    const viewW = this.scrollViewEl.clientWidth || this.viewportWidth;
+839|
+840|    if (targetY < this.scrollViewEl.scrollTop) {
+841|      this.scrollViewEl.scrollTop = targetY - 48;
+842|    } else if (targetY + this.lineHeight > this.scrollViewEl.scrollTop + 
viewH) {
+843|      this.scrollViewEl.scrollTop = targetY - viewH + this.lineHeight + 48;
+844|    }
+845|
+846|    if (targetX < this.scrollViewEl.scrollLeft) {
+847|      this.scrollViewEl.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, targetX - 48);
+848|    } else if (targetX > this.scrollViewEl.scrollLeft + viewW - 60) {
+849|      this.scrollViewEl.scrollLeft = targetX - viewW + 100;
+850|    }
+851|  }
+852|
+853|  getPosFromCoords(clientX, clientY) {
+854|    const contentRect = this.viewContentEl.getBoundingClientRect();
+855|    const relX = clientX - contentRect.left;
+856|    const relY = clientY - contentRect.top;
+857|
+858|    const line = Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.floor(relY / this.lineHeight), 
this.lines.length - 1));
+859|    const lineText = this.lines[line] || '';
+860|    const approxCol = Math.round(relX / this.charWidth);
+861|    const col = Math.max(0, Math.min(approxCol, lineText.length));
+862|
+863|    return { line, col };
+864|  }
+865|
+866|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+867|     Rendering
+868|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+869|  render() {
+870|    this.viewportHeight = this.scrollViewEl.clientHeight || 600;
+871|    this.viewportWidth = this.scrollViewEl.clientWidth || 800;
+872|
+873|    const { startLine, endLine } = this.getVisibleRange();
+874|
+875|    // 1. Render Gutter (Line Numbers)
+876|    const gutterHtml = [];
+877|    for (let i = startLine; i <= endLine; i++) {
+878|      const top = i * this.lineHeight;
+879|      const isCurrentLine = this.carets.some(c => c.head.line === i);
+880|      const activeClass = isCurrentLine ? ' active' : '';
+881|      gutterHtml.push(`
+882|        <div class="patchpad-gutter-line${activeClass}" 
style="top:${top}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px;" data-line="${i}">
+883|          ${i + 1}
+884|        </div>
+885|      `);
+886|    }
+887|    this.gutterContentEl.innerHTML = gutterHtml.join('');
+888|
+889|    // 2. Render Lines
+890|    const linesHtml = [];
+891|    for (let i = startLine; i <= endLine; i++) {
+892|      const top = i * this.lineHeight;
+893|      const text = this.lines[i] || '';
+894|      const safeText = text.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, 
'&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;') || '&nbsp;';
+895|      linesHtml.push(`
+896|        <div class="patchpad-line" 
style="top:${top}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px;">${safeText}</div>
+897|      `);
+898|    }
+899|    this.linesLayerEl.innerHTML = linesHtml.join('');
+900|
+901|    // 3. Render Selections
+902|    this.renderSelections(startLine, endLine);
+903|
+904|    // 4. Render Carets
+905|    this.renderCarets();
+906|
+907|    // 5. Render Find Highlights
+908|    this.renderFindHighlights();
+909|  }
+910|
+911|  renderSelections(startLine, endLine) {
+912|    const selHtml = [];
+913|
+914|    for (const caret of this.carets) {
+915|      if (!this.hasSelection(caret)) continue;
+916|      const { start, end } = this.getNormalizedCaret(caret);
+917|
+918|      const renderStartLine = Math.max(start.line, startLine);
+919|      const renderEndLine = Math.min(end.line, endLine);
+920|
+921|      for (let l = renderStartLine; l <= renderEndLine; l++) {
+922|        const lineText = this.lines[l] || '';
+923|        const lineStartCol = (l === start.line) ? start.col : 0;
+924|        const lineEndCol = (l === end.line) ? end.col : lineText.length;
+925|
+926|        const top = l * this.lineHeight;
+927|        const left = lineStartCol * this.charWidth;
+928|        const width = Math.max((lineEndCol - lineStartCol) * 
this.charWidth, 6);
+929|
+930|        selHtml.push(`
+931|          <div class="patchpad-selection-rect" 
style="top:${top}px;left:${left}px;width:${width}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px;
"></div>
+932|        `);
+933|      }
+934|    }
+935|
+936|    this.selectionLayerEl.innerHTML = selHtml.join('');
+937|  }
+938|
+939|  renderCarets() {
+940|    const caretsHtml = [];
+941|    const isFocused = document.activeElement === this.container;
+942|
+943|    for (let i = 0; i < this.carets.length; i++) {
+944|      const caret = this.carets[i];
+945|      const top = caret.head.line * this.lineHeight;
+946|      const left = caret.head.col * this.charWidth;
+947|      const blinkClass = (this.caretVisible && isFocused) ? ' visible' : 
'';
+948|      const isPrimary = i === 0 ? ' primary' : ' secondary';
+949|
+950|      caretsHtml.push(`
+951|        <div class="patchpad-caret${blinkClass}${isPrimary}" 
style="top:${top}px;left:${left}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px;"></div>
+952|      `);
+953|    }
+954|
+955|    this.caretsLayerEl.innerHTML = caretsHtml.join('');
+956|  }
+957|
+958|  renderFindHighlights() {
+959|    if (this.findMatchesList.length === 0) {
+960|      this.findLayerEl.innerHTML = '';
+961|      return;
+962|    }
+963|
+964|    const { startLine, endLine } = this.getVisibleRange();
+965|    const highlights = [];
+966|
+967|    for (let i = 0; i < this.findMatchesList.length; i++) {
+968|      const match = this.findMatchesList[i];
+969|      if (match.line < startLine || match.line > endLine) continue;
+970|
+971|      const top = match.line * this.lineHeight;
+972|      const left = match.startCol * this.charWidth;
+973|      const width = (match.endCol - match.startCol) * this.charWidth;
+974|      const isCurrent = i === this.activeFindIndex ? ' active-match' : '';
+975|
+976|      highlights.push(`
+977|        <div class="patchpad-find-match${isCurrent}" 
style="top:${top}px;left:${left}px;width:${width}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px;
"></div>
+978|      `);
+979|    }
+980|
+981|    this.findLayerEl.innerHTML = highlights.join('');
+982|  }
+983|
+984|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+985|     Caret Blinking
+986|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+987|  startCaretBlink() {
+988|    if (this.caretBlinkTimer) clearInterval(this.caretBlinkTimer);
+989|    this.caretBlinkTimer = setInterval(() => {
+990|      this.caretVisible = !this.caretVisible;
+991|      const carets = 
this.caretsLayerEl.querySelectorAll('.patchpad-caret');
+992|      carets.forEach(c => {
+993|        if (this.caretVisible) c.classList.add('visible');
+994|        else c.classList.remove('visible');
+995|      });
+996|    }, 530);
+997|  }
+998|
+999|  resetCaretBlink() {
+1000|    this.caretVisible = true;
+1001|    const carets = this.caretsLayerEl.querySelectorAll('.patchpad-caret');
+1002|    carets.forEach(c => c.classList.add('visible'));
+1003|  }
+1004|
+1005|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+1006|     Event Listeners
+1007|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+1008|  bindEvents() {
+1009|    // Scroll listener
+1010|    this.scrollViewEl.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
+1011|      this.scrollTop = this.scrollViewEl.scrollTop;
+1012|      this.scrollLeft = this.scrollViewEl.scrollLeft;
+1013|      this.gutterEl.scrollTop = this.scrollTop;
+1014|      this.render();
+1015|    }, { passive: true });
+1016|
+1017|    // Focus & Blur
+1018|    this.container.addEventListener('focus', () => {
+1019|      this.container.classList.add('focused');
+1020|      this.resetCaretBlink();
+1021|      this.renderCarets();
+1022|    });
+1023|
+1024|    this.container.addEventListener('blur', () => {
+1025|      this.container.classList.remove('focused');
+1026|      this.renderCarets();
+1027|    });
+1028|
+1029|    // Keyboard events
+1030|    this.container.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => 
this.handleKeyDown(e));
+1031|
+1032|    // Mouse events on editing surface
+1033|    this.scrollViewEl.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => 
this.handleMouseDown(e));
+1034|    window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => this.handleMouseMove(e));
+1035|    window.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => this.handleMouseUp(e));
+1036|
+1037|    // Gutter click (select whole line)
+1038|    this.gutterEl.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
+1039|      const lineEl = e.target.closest('.patchpad-gutter-line');
+1040|      if (lineEl) {
+1041|        const lineIdx = parseInt(lineEl.getAttribute('data-line'), 10);
+1042|        if (!isNaN(lineIdx)) {
+1043|          this.carets = [{
+1044|            anchor: { line: lineIdx, col: 0 },
+1045|            head: { line: lineIdx, col: this.lines[lineIdx].length }
+1046|          }];
+1047|          this.container.focus();
+1048|          this.render();
+1049|          this.notifyCursor();
+1050|        }
+1051|      }
+1052|    });
+1053|
+1054|    // Double click (select word) and Triple click (select line)
+1055|    this.scrollViewEl.addEventListener('dblclick', (e) => {
+1056|      const pos = this.getPosFromCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);
+1057|      const line = this.lines[pos.line] || '';
+1058|      const startCol = this.prevWordCol(line, pos.col);
+1059|      const endCol = this.nextWordCol(line, pos.col);
+1060|      this.carets = [{
+1061|        anchor: { line: pos.line, col: startCol },
+1062|        head: { line: pos.line, col: endCol }
+1063|      }];
+1064|      this.render();
+1065|      this.notifyCursor();
+1066|    });
+1067|
+1068|    // Clipboard events
+1069|    this.container.addEventListener('copy', (e) => this.handleCopy(e));
+1070|    this.container.addEventListener('cut', (e) => this.handleCut(e));
+1071|    this.container.addEventListener('paste', (e) => this.handlePaste(e));
+1072|
+1073|    // Window resize observer
+1074|    if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined') {
+1075|      new ResizeObserver(() => {
+1076|        this.measureFont();
+1077|        this.updateScrollPhantom();
+1078|        this.render();
+1079|      }).observe(this.container);
+1080|    }
+1081|  }
+1082|
+1083|  handleKeyDown(e) {
+1084|    const isMac = /Mac|iPod|iPhone|iPad/.test(navigator.platform);
+1085|    const modKey = isMac ? e.metaKey : e.ctrlKey;
+1086|    const altKey = e.altKey;
+1087|    const shiftKey = e.shiftKey;
+1088|
+1089|    this.resetCaretBlink();
+1090|
+1091|    // 1. Escape key handling
+1092|    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+1093|      e.preventDefault();
+1094|      this.options.onEscape();
+1095|      return;
+1096|    }
+1097|
+1098|    // 2. Find shortcut (Ctrl+F / Cmd+F)
+1099|    if (modKey && (e.key === 'f' || e.key === 'F')) {
+1100|      e.preventDefault();
+1101|      this.options.onFindRequest();
+1102|      return;
+1103|    }
+1104|
+1105|    // 3. Save shortcut (Ctrl+S / Cmd+S)
+1106|    if (modKey && (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S')) {
+1107|      e.preventDefault();
+1108|      this.options.onSaveRequest();
+1109|      return;
+1110|    }
+1111|
+1112|    // 4. Undo / Redo shortcuts
+1113|    if (modKey && (e.key === 'z' || e.key === 'Z')) {
+1114|      e.preventDefault();
+1115|      if (shiftKey) this.redo();
+1116|      else this.undo();
+1117|      return;
+1118|    }
+1119|    if (modKey && (e.key === 'y' || e.key === 'Y')) {
+1120|      e.preventDefault();
+1121|      this.redo();
+1122|      return;
+1123|    }
+1124|
+1125|    // 5. Select All (Ctrl+A / Cmd+A)
+1126|    if (modKey && (e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A')) {
+1127|      e.preventDefault();
+1128|      this.selectAll();
+1129|      return;
+1130|    }
+1131|
+1132|    // 6. Navigation
+1133|    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
+1134|      e.preventDefault();
+1135|      const isWord = altKey || (modKey && !isMac);
+1136|      const isLineEdge = modKey && isMac;
+1137|      this.moveCaret('left', { shift: shiftKey, word: isWord, lineEdge: 
isLineEdge });
+1138|      return;
+1139|    }
+1140|    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
+1141|      e.preventDefault();
+1142|      const isWord = altKey || (modKey && !isMac);
+1143|      const isLineEdge = modKey && isMac;
+1144|      this.moveCaret('right', { shift: shiftKey, word: isWord, lineEdge: 
isLineEdge });
+1145|      return;
+1146|    }
+1147|    if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
+1148|      e.preventDefault();
+1149|      const isDoc = modKey && isMac;
+1150|      this.moveCaret('up', { shift: shiftKey, doc: isDoc });
+1151|      return;
+1152|    }
+1153|    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
+1154|      e.preventDefault();
+1155|      const isDoc = modKey && isMac;
+1156|      this.moveCaret('down', { shift: shiftKey, doc: isDoc });
+1157|      return;
+1158|    }
+1159|    if (e.key === 'Home') {
+1160|      e.preventDefault();
+1161|      this.moveCaret('left', { shift: shiftKey, doc: modKey, lineEdge: 
!modKey });
+1162|      return;
+1163|    }
+1164|    if (e.key === 'End') {
+1165|      e.preventDefault();
+1166|      this.moveCaret('right', { shift: shiftKey, doc: modKey, lineEdge: 
!modKey });
+1167|      return;
+1168|    }
+1169|    if (e.key === 'PageUp') {
+1170|      e.preventDefault();
+1171|      for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) this.moveCaret('up', { shift: shiftKey 
});
+1172|      return;
+1173|    }
+1174|    if (e.key === 'PageDown') {
+1175|      e.preventDefault();
+1176|      for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) this.moveCaret('down', { shift: 
shiftKey });
+1177|      return;
+1178|    }
+1179|
+1180|    // 7. Tab / Shift+Tab (Indentation)
+1181|    if (e.key === 'Tab') {
+1182|      e.preventDefault();
+1183|      if (shiftKey) this.unindent();
+1184|      else this.indent();
+1185|      return;
+1186|    }
+1187|
+1188|    // 8. Enter
+1189|    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
+1190|      e.preventDefault();
+1191|      this.insertText('\n', 'type');
+1192|      return;
+1193|    }
+1194|
+1195|    // 9. Backspace / Delete
+1196|    if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
+1197|      e.preventDefault();
+1198|      this.deleteBackward();
+1199|      return;
+1200|    }
+1201|    if (e.key === 'Delete') {
+1202|      e.preventDefault();
+1203|      this.deleteForward();
+1204|      return;
+1205|    }
+1206|
+1207|    // 10. Printable characters
+1208|    if (e.key.length === 1 && !modKey && !altKey) {
+1209|      e.preventDefault();
+1210|      this.insertText(e.key, 'type');
+1211|      return;
+1212|    }
+1213|  }
+1214|
+1215|  handleMouseDown(e) {
+1216|    if (e.button !== 0) return; // Left button only
+1217|
+1218|    const isMac = /Mac|iPod|iPhone|iPad/.test(navigator.platform);
+1219|    const isMultiCaret = e.altKey || (isMac ? e.metaKey : e.ctrlKey);
+1220|
+1221|    const pos = this.getPosFromCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);
+1222|    this.container.focus();
+1223|
+1224|    if (isMultiCaret) {
+1225|      // Add or remove caret
+1226|      const existingIdx = this.carets.findIndex(
+1227|        c => c.head.line === pos.line && c.head.col === pos.col
+1228|      );
+1229|      if (existingIdx >= 0 && this.carets.length > 1) {
+1230|        this.carets.splice(existingIdx, 1);
+1231|        this.activeCaretIdx = Math.max(0, this.carets.length - 1);
+1232|      } else {
+1233|        this.carets.push({ anchor: { ...pos }, head: { ...pos } });
+1234|        this.activeCaretIdx = this.carets.length - 1;
+1235|      }
+1236|    } else {
+1237|      this.carets = [{ anchor: { ...pos }, head: { ...pos } }];
+1238|      this.activeCaretIdx = 0;
+1239|    }
+1240|
+1241|    this.isMouseDown = true;
+1242|    this.dragStartPos = { ...pos };
+1243|    this.resetCaretBlink();
+1244|    this.render();
+1245|    this.notifyCursor();
+1246|  }
+1247|
+1248|  handleMouseMove(e) {
+1249|    if (!this.isMouseDown) return;
+1250|
+1251|    const pos = this.getPosFromCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);
+1252|    const activeCaret = this.carets[this.activeCaretIdx];
+1253|    if (activeCaret) {
+1254|      activeCaret.head = { ...pos };
+1255|      this.render();
+1256|      this.notifyCursor();
+1257|    }
+1258|
+1259|    // Auto-scroll when dragging near viewport boundaries
+1260|    const rect = this.scrollViewEl.getBoundingClientRect();
+1261|    const edgeBuffer = 30;
+1262|    if (e.clientY < rect.top + edgeBuffer) {
+1263|      this.scrollViewEl.scrollTop -= 15;
+1264|    } else if (e.clientY > rect.bottom - edgeBuffer) {
+1265|      this.scrollViewEl.scrollTop += 15;
+1266|    }
+1267|  }
+1268|
+1269|  handleMouseUp() {
+1270|    this.isMouseDown = false;
+1271|    this.dragStartPos = null;
+1272|  }
+1273|
+1274|  handleCopy(e) {
+1275|    const selectedText = this.getAllSelectedText();
+1276|    if (!selectedText) return;
+1277|
+1278|    if (e.clipboardData) {
+1279|      e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', selectedText);
+1280|      e.preventDefault();
+1281|    } else if (navigator.clipboard) {
+1282|      navigator.clipboard.writeText(selectedText);
+1283|    }
+1284|  }
+1285|
+1286|  handleCut(e) {
+1287|    const selectedText = this.getAllSelectedText();
+1288|    if (!selectedText) return;
+1289|
+1290|    if (e.clipboardData) {
+1291|      e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', selectedText);
+1292|      e.preventDefault();
+1293|    } else if (navigator.clipboard) {
+1294|      navigator.clipboard.writeText(selectedText);
+1295|    }
+1296|
+1297|    this.deleteBackward();
+1298|  }
+1299|
+1300|  handlePaste(e) {
+1301|    e.preventDefault();
+1302|    let pastedText = '';
+1303|    if (e.clipboardData) {
+1304|      pastedText = e.clipboardData.getData('text/plain');
+1305|    }
+1306|
+1307|    if (pastedText) {
+1308|      this.insertText(pastedText, 'paste');
+1309|    }
+1310|  }
+1311|
+1312|  /* ----------------------------------------------------
+1313|     Change Notifications
+1314|     ---------------------------------------------------- */
+1315|  notifyChange() {
+1316|    this.options.onDirtyChange(this.getText());
+1317|    this.notifyCursor();
+1318|  }
+1319|
+1320|  notifyCursor() {
+1321|    const primary = this.carets[0] || { head: { line: 0, col: 0 } };
+1322|    this.options.onCursorChange({
+1323|      line: primary.head.line + 1,
+1324|      col: primary.head.col + 1,
+1325|      caretCount: this.carets.length,
+1326|      hasSelection: this.hasSelection(primary),
+1327|      selectedLength: this.getSelectedText(primary).length
+1328|    });
+1329|  }
+1330|
+1331|  focus() {
+1332|    this.container.focus();
+1333|    this.resetCaretBlink();
+1334|    this.renderCarets();
+1335|  }
+1336|}
+1337|
+1338|window.PatchPadEditor = PatchPadEditor;
+1339|
[end of edit 1 / 1]

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create style.css for PatchPad UI

Reasoning:
**Analyzing Requirements and Planning Implementation**

Okay, let's break down these requirements from `editing.md`, `persistence.md`, 
`conflict-safety.md`, and `interface.md` and see how they map onto the 
`editor.js` and, more broadly, the application structure. It seems we have a lot
to cover.

First, **`editing.md`** has a detailed set of features. We need to focus on 
proper keyboard navigation – especially around the "Find" input with the Escape 
key. Emoji and accented text support should be robust. I'll need to make sure 
the text selection and editing mechanics, including copy/cut/paste and 
undo/redo, are on par with standard document editors. Supporting multiple carets
and ensuring uninterrupted typing within a single undo/redo action are key.

Next, **`persistence.md`** focuses on find/replace functionality, indicating 
unsaved changes, revision history with previews and safe restoration, which will
require careful implementation for revision handling, including tracking 
changes, showing revision numbers and timestamps and restoring older revisions 
in an undoable state. The find and replace functionality needs to be 
comprehensive, including displaying the number of matches.

**`conflict-safety.md`** is critical, requiring a robust conflict resolution 
strategy. We need to prevent stale saves based on the server's version, keep the
rejected tab's content, and provide clear messaging. The server-side validation 
is vital. I'll have to develop error handling to validate incoming document 
requests.

Finally, **`interface.md`** outlines the user experience. The application must 
be usable in a standard browser, with clear feedback on actions and errors. The 
report title needs to be persistently visible. The design demands discoverable 
controls, readable labels, keyboard focus, and a coherent visual style with 
readable typography, clear contrast, and consistent spacing across all 
components and history entries. It has to be readable at desktop size, with 
overflow handled gracefully.

Now I see the roadmap for building.

1.  **`public/css/style.css`**: It will be a clean, high-contrast, modern UI 
layout.
2.  **`public/js/app.js`**: I'll create the UI controller, linking the editor, 
API, toolbar, find/replace bar, revision history drawer, preview modal, and 
conflict dialog.
3.  **`public/index.html`**: The semantic HTML structure will be the foundation.

Okay, let's start writing `public/css/style.css`.

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create index.html for PatchPad UI

Reasoning:
**My Plan for the UI**

Okay, time to build the UI! First things first: I need to get the basic 
structure set up. I'll start by creating `public/index.html` and 
`public/js/app.js`. Then I'll dive into the detailed requirements to ensure a 
solid user experience. I have to make sure everything lines up with the 
specifications in `interface.md`, `editing.md`, `persistence.md`, and 
`conflict-safety.md`.

Here's the checklist in my mind:

*   **Document Title:** The title must be "Northwind API Incident Report" and 
stay visible when the report is open. It's a key piece of visual context.
*   **Report Selection:** The user needs a clear way to see and choose reports. 
I think a dropdown or a list with the ID, title, and author will work best.
*   **Control Panel:** These controls are essential for effective report 
manipulation.
    *   **Save:** There absolutely needs to be a "Save" button and shortcut.
    *   **Undo/Redo:** Undo and Redo buttons, along with shortcut hints, are 
non-negotiable for efficient editing.
    *   **Find and Replace:** A Find and Replace toggle, and bar are must haves.
I'll need to think about that, Find input, match count, Find Next, Find Prev, 
Replace input, Replace Current, Replace All.
    *   **History Toggle:** This is vital. I need to make sure I can display 
report history.
*   **Escape Key Handling:** I must make sure the Escape key has clear behavior 
when editing and using the Find bar. I need to handle focus appropriately.
*   **Badges:** I can't overlook the importance of these for providing real-time
feedback:
    *   **Save State:** I need a clear indicator with "Saved" or "Unsaved 
changes".
    *   **Current Revision:** A simple "Revision X" badge is crucial.
    *   **Cursor Position:** "Ln X, Col Y" will keep the user informed.
*   **Revision History Drawer:**
    *   This is key. I'll need to display a revision list with timestamps and 
numbers. I also want Preview and Restore options. Preview should display the 
text as read-only.
*   **Conflict Handling:** Conflict resolution is key to this application:
    *   I want a useful conflict banner and/or dialog when a 409 error occurs.
    *   The user's unsaved draft must be preserved.
    *   I'll need to provide options: "Keep Draft", "Reload Server Version", and
"Compare".

Okay, now let's write `public/index.html`.
